- **advisor-detective**: Perform evidence-backed background research on user-selected academic advisors after Advisor Finder. Use when the user asks to investigate, compare, or backcheck specific advisors. Research only explicitly selected information sections, preserve exact advisor-program identities, optionally consult consented local community sources, and report supported findings, conflicts, risks, and information gaps without treating anonymous claims as facts. ([Captain23333/find-your-perfect-advisor](https://github.com/Captain23333/find-your-perfect-advisor/tree/HEAD/skills/advisor-detective) · ★79)
- **advisor-detective**: 导师深度调查与套磁情报技能（Advisor Detective）。在 advisor-finder 完成导师匹配排序后，对 Top N 位导师调查近三年论文内容、主页近期项目、学术社交动态和学生评价。 所有深度均检查论文摘要与可访问的结论、个人/实验室主页、小红书、X/Twitter 和 ResearchGate；medium/high 进一步交叉验证导师人品、学术潜力和重大动态。 输出维度对齐的结构化 Excel、证据链接、近期研究主线和可用于个性化联系邮件的具体切入点。 触发场景：advisor-finder 完成后，用户想对候选导师进行深度背调；或用户说"调查导师"、"背调导师"、"查一下这个导师"。 ([Ztang-Yit-Xiaang/GradSchoolApplication](https://github.com/Ztang-Yit-Xiaang/GradSchoolApplication/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/advisor-detective))

- **adyen-invalid-browserinfo-provided-3ds-9518c21acc3664e9**: Fixes Adyen 3DS2 error 15_015 Invalid browserInfo provided. Covers sending the complete browserInfo object with all eight fields, using correct JSON types for numeric and boolean fields, valid colorDepth values, and a real acceptHeader instead of a hardcoded string. Not for native app flows or the colorDepth-specific 15_018. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/adyen-invalid-browserinfo-provided-3ds-9518c21acc3664e9))
- **adyen-invalid-card-number-error-101-b75f68db91d6a9a3**: Fixes Adyen error 101 Invalid card number on /payments calls. Covers sanitizing the input, checking digit length against the card brand, running a Luhn check before sending, and switching to Adyen's secured card component with client-side encryption instead of custom card forms. Not for error 102 (unknown BIN) or genuine issuer declines. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/adyen-invalid-card-number-error-101-b75f68db91d6a9a3))
- **adyen-invalid-hmac-signature-on-webhoo-c7f8dec754d80048**: Fixes Adyen webhook handlers that reject notifications with Invalid HMAC signature. Covers using the HMAC key from the correct webhook configuration, verifying the signature over the raw request bytes instead of re-serialized JSON, the 10-minute propagation window after key rotation, and the different header-based signing used by non-standard webhooks. Not for notifications that arrive with no signature at all. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/adyen-invalid-hmac-signature-on-webhoo-c7f8dec754d80048))
- **adyen-invalid-merchant-account-error-9-fb0e02e2100652b7**: Fixes Adyen error 901 Invalid Merchant Account. Covers copying the case-sensitive merchant account name exactly from the Customer Area, matching it to the right environment, confirming the web service user is permitted for the account, and not confusing the 3DS MD value with merchantAccount. Not for 010 Not allowed or genuine credential problems. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/adyen-invalid-merchant-account-error-9-fb0e02e2100652b7))
- **adyen-invalid-paymentdata-checkout-14-89791ee0eafe2516**: Fixes Adyen Checkout error 14_003 Invalid paymentData on /payments/details calls. Explains that paymentData is single-use and rotated every step, the fix of always forwarding the paymentData from the latest action response, and keeping paymentData and MD paired from the same attempt. Not for missing paymentData (14_002) or first-call /payments failures. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/adyen-invalid-paymentdata-checkout-14-89791ee0eafe2516))
- **adyen-invalid-threeds2token-3ds2-15-01-50a6bcf3261457fe**: Fixes Adyen 3DS2 error 15_014 Invalid threeDS2Token. Explains the token is single-use and bound to one transaction, the fix of never caching or replaying tokens across attempts and restarting from /payments on failure, and completing fingerprint and challenge promptly. Not for missing tokens (15_009) or the 12-hour transaction window (15_017). ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/adyen-invalid-threeds2token-3ds2-15-01-50a6bcf3261457fe))
